# ​C5.1.4 op0 == 0b01, cache maintenance, TLB maintenance, address translation, prediction restriction, BRBE, Trace Extension, Guarded Control Stack, and FEAT_RME_GPC3 instructions

Source: <https://developer.arm.com/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions>

##### C5.1.4 op0 == `0b01`, cache maintenance, TLB maintenance, address translation, prediction restriction, BRBE, Trace Extension, Guarded Control Stack, and FEAT\_RME\_GPC3 instructions

The System instructions are encoded with `op0`== `0b01`. The different groups of System instructions are identified by the values of `CRn` and `CRm`, except that some of this encoding space is reserved for IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED functionality. The encoding of these instructions is:

![](images/0415-C5.1.4-op0-0b01-cache-maintenance-TLB-maintenance-address-translation-prediction-restriction-BRBE-Trace-Extension-Guarded-Control-Stack-and-FEAT_RME_G-img01.svg)

The grouping of these instructions depending on the `CRn` and `CRm` fields is as follows:

`CRn` == `0b0111`
:   The instruction group is determined by the value of `CRm`, as follows:

    `CRm` == `0b0000`
    :   Associate a physical address with a memory-mapped location, APAS instruction.

        See [FEAT\_RME\_GPC3 instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#sys_instr_enc_gpc3).

    `CRm` == { `0b0001`, `0b0101`}
    :   Instruction cache maintenance instructions.

        See [Cache maintenance instructions, and data cache zero operations](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#babjchdd).

    `CRm` == `0b0010`
    :   Branch Record Buffer and Trace Extension instructions.

        See [Branch Record Buffer instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#cihfabdgh2) and [Trace Extension instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#beihbjeah6).

    `CRm` == `0b0011`
    :   Prediction restriction instructions.

        See [Prediction restriction instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#cihjahda).

    `CRm` == `0b0100`
    :   Data cache zero operations.

        See [Cache maintenance instructions, and data cache zero operations](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#babjchdd).

    `CRm` == { `0b0110`, `0b1010`, `0b1011`, `0b1100`, `0b1101`, `0b1110`, `0b1111`}
    :   Data cache maintenance instructions.

        See [Cache maintenance instructions, and data cache zero operations](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#babjchdd).

    `CRm` == `0b0111`
    :   Guarded Control Stack instructions.

        See [Guarded Control Stack instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#beibccehi6).

    `CRm` == { `0b1000`, `0b1001`}
    :   See [Address translation instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#babbcbce).

`CRn` == { `0b1000`, `0b1001`}
:   See [TLB maintenance instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#babfhggc).

`CRn` == { `0b1011`, `0b1111`}
:   See [Reserved encoding space for IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED instructions](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#babeiaih).

###### C5.1.4.1 Cache maintenance instructions, and data cache zero operations

[Table C5-1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#tbl_aarch64_register_encoding_table_ic_op001_dc_op001) lists the Cache maintenance instructions and their encodings. Instructions that take an argument include `Xt` in the instruction syntax. For instructions that do not take an argument, the Rt field is encoded as `0b11111`. For these instructions, if the Rt field is not set to `0b11111`, it is CONSTRAINED UNPREDICTABLE whether:

- The instruction is UNDEFINED.
- The instruction behaves as if the Rt field is set to `0b11111`.

###### C5.1.4.2 Prediction restriction instructions

[Table C5-2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#tbl_aarch64_register_encoding_table_pred_instrs) lists the Prediction restriction instructions and their encodings. Instructions that take an argument include `Xt` in the instruction syntax.

<table id="tbl_aarch64_register_encoding_table_pred_instrs">
 <caption>
  Table C5-2 Prediction restriction instructions
 </caption>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>

  <col/>
 </colgroup>

   <th>

     op0
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op1
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>

    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>

    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>

    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>

    </strong>

###### C5.1.4.4 TLB maintenance instructions

[Table C5-4](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-1-The-System-instruction-class-encoding-space/-C5-1-4-op0----0b01--cache-maintenance--TLB-maintenance--address-translation--prediction-restriction--BRBE--Trace-Extension--Guarded-Control-Stack--and-FEAT-RME-GPC3-instructions?lang=en#tbl_aarch64_register_encoding_table_tlbi_op001) lists the TLB maintenance instructions and their encodings. Instructions that take an argument include `Xt` in the instruction syntax. For instructions that do not take an argument, the Rt field is encoded as `0b11111`. For these instructions, if the Rt field is not set to `0b11111`, it is CONSTRAINED UNPREDICTABLE whether:

- The instruction is UNDEFINED.
- The instruction behaves as if the Rt field is set to `0b11111`.

###### C5.1.4.9 Reserved encoding space for IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED instructions

The A64 instruction set reserves the following encoding space for IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED instructions:

![](images/0415-C5.1.4-op0-0b01-cache-maintenance-TLB-maintenance-address-translation-prediction-restriction-BRBE-Trace-Extension-Guarded-Control-Stack-and-FEAT_RME_G-img02.svg)

The value of `L` defines the use of `Rt` as follows:

0
:   `Rt` is an argument supplied to the instruction.

1
:   `Rt` is a result returned by the instruction.

IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED instructions in this encoding space are accessed using the `SYS`, `SYSL`, and `SYSP` instructions, see [SYS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C6-A64-Base-Instruction-Descriptions/-C6-2-Alphabetical-list-of-A64-base-instructions/-C6-2-475-SYS?lang=en#isa_sys), [SYSL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C6-A64-Base-Instruction-Descriptions/-C6-2-Alphabetical-list-of-A64-base-instructions/-C6-2-476-SYSL?lang=en#isa_sysl) and [SYSP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C6-A64-Base-Instruction-Descriptions/-C6-2-Alphabetical-list-of-A64-base-instructions/-C6-2-477-SYSP?lang=en#isa_sysp).

Accesses to any of the IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED System instruction encodings that are not trapped are permitted to be UNDEFINED.

See also [Reserved encodings for IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED registers](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D23-AArch64-System-Register-Encoding/-D23-3-Moves-to-and-from-non-debug-System-registers--Special-purpose-registers/-D23-3-2-Reserved-encodings-for-IMPLEMENTATION-DEFINED-registers?lang=en#beicjbih).
